{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"retracts","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An act of retracting or withdrawing (a mistake, a statement, etc.); a retraction."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["A pulling back, especially (military) of an army or military troops; a pull-back, a retreat; also, a signal for this to be done."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["A subgroup of a given group such that there is a surjective endomorphism from the ambient group to the subgroup which is constant on the subgroup; in this case the subgroup is a retract of the ambient group. In symbols: H in G is a retract of G if there exists a surjective homomorphism σ from G to H with σ|_H= operatorname id."],"tags":["obsolete"],"topics":["group-theory","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["The target of a retraction."],"tags":["obsolete"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences","topology"]},{"glosses":["Synonym of retreat (“an act of accidentally injuring a horse's foot by incorrectly nailing it during shoeing”)."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["synonym","synonym-of"],"word":"retreat"}],"tags":["obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɹɪˈtɹækt/","tags":["Australia","Canada","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɹɪˈtɹækt/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ɹiˈtɹækt/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ɹəˈtɹækt/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":449233,"upstream_offset":2119767176,"upstream_record_sha256":"dbb08a4065e1b45f97d7df4b5edabe12caf44c501c3ba598ca577eb594c417c4","word":"retract"}
